#  Network Services roles and permissions Stay organized with collections  Save and categorize content based on your preferences. 

This page lists the IAM roles and permissions for Network Services. To search through all roles and permissions, see the [role and permission index](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions).

## Network Services roles

---|---

## Network Services permissions

Permission | Included in roles  
---|---  
  
#### `networkservices.agentGateways.create`

|  [Admin](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-overview#basic) (`roles/admin`)  [Owner](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-overview#basic) (`roles/owner`)  [Writer](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-overview#basic) (`roles/writer`)  [Editor](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-overview#basic) (`roles/editor`)  [Compute Network Admin](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/compute#compute.networkAdmin) (`roles/compute.networkAdmin`)  [Network Services Admin](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/networkservices#networkservices.admin) (`roles/networkservices.admin`)  [Network Services Editor](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/networkservices#networkservices.editor) (`roles/networkservices.editor`)  [Infrastructure Administrator](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/jobfunctions#iam.infrastructureAdmin) (`roles/iam.infrastructureAdmin`)  [Network Administrator](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/jobfunctions#iam.networkAdmin) (`roles/iam.networkAdmin`)  Service agent roles **Warning:** Don't grant service agent roles to any principals except [service agents](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/service-agents). 

  * [Cloud TPU V2 API Service Agent](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/tpu#cloudtpu.serviceAgent) (`roles/cloudtpu.serviceAgent`) 
  * [Cloud Composer API Service Agent](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/composer#composer.serviceAgent) (`roles/composer.serviceAgent`) 
  * [Kubernetes Engine Service Agent](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/container#container.serviceAgent) (`roles/container.serviceAgent`) 
  * [Cloud Dataflow Service Agent](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/dataflow#dataflow.serviceAgent) (`roles/dataflow.serviceAgent`) 

  
  
#### `networkservices.agentGateways.delete`
